機器人和半導體設備需求強勁，惟評價合理 
 1Q26 毛利率受惠產能利 用率上揚和產品結構優化，優於預期，稅後
EPS 1.64 元：上銀 1Q26 營收為 63.7 億元 ，-1.9%QoQ，+9.3%Y oY；
1Q26 區域別比重為亞洲 56%、歐洲 28%、美洲 8%、台灣 8%。產品
組合為滾珠螺桿 20%、線性滑軌 60%、機器人 12%、其他 9%。獲利
方面，毛利率 31.9%，優於預期之 29.3%，係因 1)人民幣升值影響約
1.2 個百分點、2)高毛利的歐洲佔比增加、3)產能利用率上揚，營益率
9.9%，優於預期之 9.1%，業外部份，匯兌利益挹注約 1.03 億元，稅
後 EPS 1.64 元，優於預期之 1.06 元。 
 產業谷底復甦，調整 2026 年稅後 EPS 至 8.49 元：3M26 日本工具機
訂單達 1,935 億日圓，+28.1%Y oY，累積+26%Y oY，美國市場受惠半
導體與航太需求帶動累計+48.3%Y oY，中國市場受惠汽車與自動化需
求，累計+46.2%Y oY，歐洲市場也重回成長軌道，累計+19.9%Y oY，
2026 年工具機市場已進入景氣回升階段。展望後市，公司對 2026 年
各地區看法 : 美國回升力道不錯，美國製造效應發酵，台灣工具機銷
往美國有很多機會 ， 歐洲汽車動能恢復仍不佳 ， 自動化有工業4.0 的持
續推升表現相對穩定，中國市場則因機器人、半導體、 AI 相關需求很
熱，來自中國經銷反饋，認為工具機市場到年底都很熱；機器人部門
為 2026 年成長主軸 ， 主要以1)半導體設備需求隨半導體客戶設廠挹注
營運動能；2)美系物流機器人：1H26 客戶小量投入生產線，初步金額
貢獻有限，最快 2H26 會增加出貨，總結前述，調整 2026 年營收至
285.8 億元(原 259.2 億元)，+8.5%Y oY。獲利部分，受惠訂單回籠、
產能利用率將逐步回升，加計下半年漲價效應，價量齊揚下有望推升
獲利逐季走揚，故調整 2026 年稅 EPS 至 8.49 元(原 5.58 元)。 
 營運展望正向，惟評價合理，維持中立評等，目標價 350 元：評價部
分，當前公司評價處 42x，評價偏高，故維持中立評等，目標價 350
元(41x2026 EPS)。

上銀科技為全球第二大線性滑軌
廠，滾珠螺桿市佔率全球第一 
 上銀科技為全球第二大線性滑軌廠 
上銀科技股份有限公司為全球第二大線性滑軌廠，總部位於台灣台中工業區，
主要從事精密滾珠螺桿、線性傳動元件與工業型機械人研發製造，以自有品牌
「HIWIN」行銷全球。2025 年產品別：線性滑軌 60%、滾珠螺桿 20%、機器
人 11%與其他 9%；區域別：亞洲 56%、歐洲 28%、美洲 8%、台灣 8%。

Exhibit 2:公司終端產品銷售比重

Sources : Company、MasterLink

1Q26 毛利率受惠產能利 用率上
揚和產品結構優化，稅後 EPS 
1.64 元

1Q26 毛利率受惠產能利用率上揚和產品結構優化，稅後 EPS 1.64 元 
上銀 1Q26 營收為 63.7 億元，-1.9%QoQ，+9.3%Y oY；1Q26 區域別比重為
亞洲 56%、歐洲 28%、美洲 8%、台灣 8%。產品組合為滾珠螺桿 20%、線性
滑軌 60%、機器人 12%、其他 9%。獲利方面，毛利率 31.9%，優於預期之
29.3%，係因 1)人民幣升值影響約 1.2 個百分點、2)高毛利的歐洲佔比增加、
3)產能利用率上揚，營益率 9.9%，優於預期之 9.1%，業外部份，匯兌利益挹
注約 1.03 億元，稅後 EPS 1.64 元，優於預期之 1.06 元。

2Q26 營運展望正向 ， 訂單能見度
上看 4.5-5 個月，預估稅後 EPS 
2.11 元

2Q26 營運展望正向，訂單能見度上看 4.5-5 個月，預估稅後 EPS 2.11 元 
上銀 4 月營收為 24.7 億元，+5.0%MoM，+20.7%Y oY，營收達成率 34%，
優於預期，係因線軌與滾珠螺桿兩大產品線溫和復甦。展望後市，目前訂單能
見度滾珠螺桿 4.5-5 個月 、 線性滑軌3.5 個月 ， 農歷年至今都在加班去化訂單 ，
公司表示接下來幾個月的營收獲利不會低於 3-4 月 。 此外 ， 管理層表示反應原
物料上漲 ，3 月有調漲售價 ， 不同產品 、 不同區域漲幅不同 ， 平均漲幅10-15%。
考量產品交期長達 3-4 個月， 預計漲價效益在2Q26 季底才會顯現， 故元富調
整 2Q26 營收至 71.7 億元(原 61.3 億元)，+12.4%QoQ，+21%Y oY。獲利部
分，在產能利用率上升，且費用支出較為固定下，對毛利看法正向，不考慮一
次性業外損益下，調整稅後 EPS 至 2.11 元(原 0.96 元)。

產業谷底復甦 ， 調整2026 年稅後
EPS 至 8.49 元 
 產業谷底復甦，調整 2026 年稅後 EPS 至 8.49 元 
3M26 日本工具機訂單達 1,935 億日圓，+31.9%MoM，+28.1%Y oY，累積
+26%Y oY，美國市場受惠半導體與航太需求帶動累計+48.3%Y oY，中國市場
受惠於汽車與自動化需求，累計 +46.2%Y oY，歐洲市場也重回成長軌道，累
計+19.9%Y oY，2026 年工具機市場已進入景氣回升階段。展望後市，公司對
2026 年各地區看法 : 美國回升力道不錯，川普美國製造效應發酵，台灣工具
機銷往美國有很多機會，歐洲汽車動能回復仍不佳，自動化需求有工業 4.0 的
持續推升表現相對穩定，中國市場則因機器人、半導體、 AI 相關需求很熱，
來自中國經銷反饋，認為中國工具機市場到年底都很熱；機器人部門為 2026
年成長主軸 ， 主要以1)半導體設備(晶圓機器人何晶圓移載系統)需求隨半導體
客戶設廠挹注營運動能；2)美系物流機器人：1H26 客戶小量投入生產線，初
步金額貢獻有限，最快 2H26 會逐步增加出貨，總結前述，調整 2026 年營收
至 285.8 億元(原 259.2 億元)，+8.5%Y oY。獲利部分，受惠訂單回籠、產能
利用率將逐步回升，加計下半 年漲價效應，價量齊揚下有望推升獲利逐季走
揚，故調整 2026 年稅 EPS 至 8.49 元(原 5.58 元)。

營運展望正向，惟評價合理，維
持中立評等，目標價 350 元 
 營運展望正向，惟評價合理，維持中立評等，目標價 350 元 
管理層對 2026 年營運展望正向，且 AI 資料中心的興起帶動技術革新，新製程
採用帶動眾多電子行業的擴廠動作、設備採購，設備零件、次系統或自動化配
套需求明顯增加。過往評價區間處 18-40x 水準，當前公司評價處 42x，評價偏
高，故維持中立評等，目標價 350 元(41x2026 EPS)。

WPG Holdings (3702.TW) From To
Price Target NT$121.00 NT$160.00
WPG reported strong 1Q earnings:    WPG reported its 1Q results. The 1Q revenue
was NT$316.5bn, +24%     Q/Q, +27% Y/Y, in line with MSe. Revenue was driven by AI
infra     build-out and semiconductors demand, and expanded supply chain     services

(e.g., LaaS). 1Q gross profit was NT$14.2bn, gross margin was 4.5%, +0.2ppt Q/Q and
+0.8ppt Y/Y, 0.2ppt above our forecast. 1Q operating profit was NT$8.5bn, with
operating margin at 2.7%, +0.5ppt Q/Q and  +1.0ppt Y/Y, 0.2ppt above our estimate.
1Q (diluted)     EPS was NT$3.17, +95% Q/Q and +192% Y/Y, 12% above our estimate.
ROWC reached     15.2%, up from 11.5% in 4Q25. This follows the strong 1Q results of
WT Micro: Scale drives operating leverage

.
Strong 2Q guidance by WPG: The company guided for mid-point 2Q revenue of NT
$355bn, gross margin of     4.35%, operating margin of 2.58% and (non-diluted) EPS of
NT$3.86. The strong     outlook is driven by continued global AI demand and capex
guidance,     and management expects continued strong demand from inference and
edge AI.
Other highlights from WPG 1Q earnings
: By application type, compute,
communication, consumer, industrial and auto     accounted for 49%, 15%, 8%, 7% and

7%, respectively, which shows strong growth for     compute and moderate growth for
industrial and auto. By device type,     memory and core component accounted for 45%
and 23%, respectively. The reduction in     the core component mix from 31% in 4Q25
represents the company's     drive to improve margins.
The AI story lifts earnings for both WT and WPG:
Based on the actual 1Q results
for WPG and strong guidance for 2Q     forward, we raise our earnings estimates:

•  WT – EPS up 12%,  17% and 16% for 2026-28, respectively;
• WPG – EPS up 32% for each 2026-28.
We raise price targets for  WT from NT$299 to NT$349 and for      WPG from NT
$121 to NT$160:    We continue to use residual income valuation models; PT changes
reflect our earnings estimate revisions.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
WPG reported strong 1Q earnings with EPS 12% above MSe.
WPG 2Q mid-point guidance implies 12% Q/Q revenue growth and 17% Q/Q EPS
growth.
WPG management remains positive on the secular AI-driven demand.

WPG: Estimate Revisions Summary
We raise 2026-28e EPS by 32% each:   This factors in the 1Q26 revenue result and 2Q26

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 061: 260521_daiwa_TSMT

Source PDF: all report pdf/260521_daiwa_TSMT.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-05-21
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Background: We met with Taiwan Surface Mounting Technology (TSMT)’s 
management on 21 May (see our 13 May flash). Per management, its 
product mix for 2025 was: TFT-LCD 46%, Mini LED 11%, DRAM module 
16%, auto 9%, cable 9%, touch 5%, and others 4%. Management guides 
for the mix for 2026 to be: TFT-LCD 46%, Mini LED 11%, DRAM module 
17%, auto 8%, cable 6%, touch 5%, and others 7%. The QFII holding of 
TSMT was 18.2% on 20 May vs 19.8% at the end of 2025.

Highlights: 2026-27 guidance. TSMT guides for YoY revenue growth of 5-
10% (close to the high end; +1.9% YoY revenue growth for 4M26) for 2026 
and a double-digit % YoY growth for 2027, thanks to the multiple revenue 
drivers from AI-related demand. For 2026, the company expects gross 
margin of 13-15% (up QoQ; vs 12.8% gross margin in 2025), with further 
YoY growth in 2027 on a more favourable product mix. Its AI-related 
revenue target is 15-20% for 2027, up from 5-6% in 4Q26 and 1-2% in 
2026. For 2026, the company expects 2H26 revenue to be higher than that 
in 1H26, thanks to the rising order outlook from DRAM modules and AI-
related revenue supported by its capacity ramping up from its Mexico, 
India, and Vietnam plants.

Multiple revenue drivers to kick off from 2H26. TSMT mentioned several 
revenue drivers as follows: 1) Optical transceiver assembly. TSMT will be 
the major supplier of 800G and 1.6T products to its US-based client, and 
the company has seen some outsourcing demand from China-based 
clients, with 4-5% of combined revenue contribution in 4Q26. 2) DRAM 
module. TSMT expects the revenue contribution to rise to 20-25% in 2H26, 
up from 18% in 2Q26, and to grow YoY in 2027 considering its US- and 
China-based clients. 3) Low earth orbit (LEO). TSMT will start delivering 
SMT products in 2H26 for a US-based client. 4) Humanoid robots. The 
company sees business opportunity (sensor modules and PCBA) from the 
US-based client with revenue contribution from 2027, likely to reach a low 
single-digit % of 2027 revenue. 5) Battery backup unit (BBU). The 
company will trial run products in 4Q26 or 2027. 6) Server SMT. Currently, 
TSMT has two clients and plans to add a new one in 2H26 from Taiwan or 
the US. Thus, the company guides for capex of USD100-150m over 2026-
27, up from USD100m in 2025, subject to its capacity expansion plan in 
Mexico, as it expects capacity constraints for SMT in the next 1-2 years 
due to the increasing use of AI applications. The company also plans to 
expand its production capacity in China, Vietnam, Taiwan, and Mexico.

Valuation: The stock is currently trading at PERs of 21.4x and 17.4x based 
on the Bloomberg consensus’ 2026E and 2027E EPS, respectively.

21 May 2026

Positive tone from management

➢ TSMT expects stronger YoY revenue growth for 2027 vs 2026 
➢ Several new drivers to underpin revenue outlook over 2026-27 
➢ AI-related revenue to reach 15-20% of 2027 revenue vs 1-2% for 2026

## Document 062: 260521_ms_apmemory

Source PDF: all report pdf/260521_ms_apmemory.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-05-21
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

M
Idea
AP Memory Technology Corp  | Asia Pacific
Too early to be concerned
about Si-Cap competition; OW
We think the market is overly concerned oabout competition in
the early cycle. Our supply/demand model suggests ongoing
supply shortage in the next three years. Time to accumulate
more.
Our further thoughts on the Si-Cap competition  (link): SEMCO (covered by
Shawn Kim) was already a player in the Si-Cap market, but we believe its recently
announced ~US$1bn contract introduces competition to AP Memory's market share.

We believe SEMCO's Si-Cap business uses a stacking technology as well and aims to
address a similar application –  i.e., EMIB; the advantage is the better foundry process
from a potential Taiwanese partner, Winbond. That said, we think it will take time to
acquire the equipment and go through qualification with substrate vendors, EMIB,
and end customers.
Our supply/demand model suggests supply shortage in the next three years: For
the non-TSM silicon capacitor market, we expect supply shortage of 57%for 2026,

16% for 2027, and 11% for 2028 (please refer to our supply/demand model: Exhibit
1 ). We see EMIB (TPU v10/v11 and potentially auto chips in the future), AI GPUs in
China, and smartphone SoC as major market opportunities for silicon capacitors.
Supply remains constrained with limited global capacity available.
Remain OW on AP Memory; VHM is the next driver: We maintain our earnings
estimates and price target at NT$1,555. In addition to Si-Cap, we think the market

overlooks another big opportunity from VHM.
Remain OW on Powerchip
: Powerchip is fully loaded with pricing up on all products
(analog, logic, memory).
Remain EW on Winbond:
We believe SEMCO will act as a fabless design company
for its new contract, leveraging Winbond as its foundry partner. We view this as
positive for Winbond, but we expect limited revenue contribution –   only a low-
single-digit pat of the  revenue mix in 2027e. Our price target is unchanged.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Winbond Electronics Corp (2344.TW)
Base case, 2.7x 2026e P/B. This method is in line with the approach we adopt for Greater China
memory IDM peers, in view of the industry's high volatility. We believe the stock merits a mul-
tiple beyond the high end of its historical band of 0.5-1.5x since 2017, as we are seeing DRAM
pricing upside (even though pricing power could moderate into 2H26) and a sustainable NOR

price hike.
Risks to Upside
n NOR Flash pricing increase, driven by stronger-than-expected demand
n Stronger-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing supply shortage
n Faster-than-expected SLC NAND development
Risks to Downside
n NOR Flash downcycle, driven by weaker demand
n Lower-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing oversupply
n Slower-than-expected SLC NAND development
AP Memory Technology Corp (6531.TW)
Base case, derived from a residual income model. Key RI model assumptions include 1) cost

of equity constant at 9.2% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.2 beta); 2) payout ratio of
67%; 3) terminal growth rate of 3.0%; and 4) medium-term growth rate of 16.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected consumer demand
n Faster-than-expected IPD ramp and WoW packaging development
n Milder competition from partners (foundry, memory house, GPU vendor) or other
design houses
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected consumer demand
n Slower-than-expected IPD ramp and WoW packaging development

n Intensifying competition from partners (foundry, memory house, GPU vendor) or other
design houses
Powerchip Semiconductor Manufacturing Co (6770.TW)
Base case, 2.1x 2026e P/B. This method is in line with our approach for Greater China memory
IDM players, in view of the industry's high volatility. Our 2.1x target P/B is higher than the
historical average of 1.5x since 2021, reflecting our optimistic view on improved pricing and
utilization.
Risks to Upside

n Pricing power is sustained in 2026.
n Listing on main board attracts investment fund flows.

n Aluminum process enjoys higher margins.
Risks to Downside
n Competition from China intensifies.
n Price erosion is faster than expected.
n Demand from consumer electronics clients weakens further.
n Inventory in the supply chain takes longer to digest.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
At ISCAS 2026 in Shanghai on May 25, Huawei introduced the "t (Tau) Law" - a time-
based scaling principle for multi-layer electronic systems that aims to succeed
Moore's Law as geometric scaling faces limitations. This approach optimizes a
unified time constant (t) across all computing levels—from transistors to data

centers—shifting focus from size reduction to overall system efficiency, including
interconnects, signal integrity, power, and thermal management.
Under this new concept, Huawei has innovated "Logic Folding" — vertically stacking
active layers for digital, analog, and memory circuits, which has achieved a 55%
increase in transistor density and a 41% gain in power efficiency at a fixed process
node.
At the AI cluster level,  t Law, combined with technologies such as a unified memory

semantic bus interconnect architecture, near-package optical Hi-ONE (in-package
high-speed optoelectronic interconnects), and edge-to-surface 3D Folding, is
projected to achieve more than a 100× increase in hardware integration by 2035.
Global AI Transceivers: Industry Demand Likely to be Even Stronger (14 May 2026)
Global Technology: AI Transceivers: Growth Dominates Disruption (26 Feb 2026)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We believe the "t (Tau) Law" is a fundamental theory supporting the exponential
growth of the AI transceiver industry. We reiterate our positive view.
t (Tau) Law is a  framework introduced by Huawei to optimize signal delay (t) to
boost transistor density, energy efficiency, and performance.

Idea
MediaTek is working closely with Intel to enable the 2nm TPU “Humufish": On its
latest earnings call, MediaTek indicated for the first time that it expects 2nm ASIC to
enter mass production by 2027, but investors remained concerned about production
volumes in 2028, as Intel's EMIB-T was not proven in a large-scale ASIC project. In
our prior report
, we assumed a conservative 2028 volume of 1mn units of  2nm TPU,
implying ~US$20bn of total AI ASIC revenue. However, our recent industry checks

suggest upside for next
‑gen 2nm TPU, "Humufish", given (1) more ABF substrate
capacity, as we believe major EMIB substrate suppliers currently include Ibiden,
Unimicron, Shinko, and AT&S, and SEMCO could be added;  (2) EMIB yield at Intel
foundry is already high (>90%), ie yield improvement from embedding technology/
TGV (Through Glass Via); and  (3) the rest of the supply chain is in place, including
bumping (PTI, etc.), silicon capacitor (
AP Memory/Semco), and silicon bridge die

(from Intel's foundry). We now expect at least 2.5mn units of  2nm TPU in 2028,
mainly based on Intel EMIB, implying at least US$37bn of total TPU revenue (2.5mn
units of 2nm TPU at US$13k ASP, and 1mn units of 3nm TPU at $4.5k ASP).
Beyond 2nm TPU, MediaTek engages in the 1.4nm TPU v10 "Icefish", supporting
stronger growth from 2029 onward: Our latest supply chain checks suggest
MediaTek is likely to secure another TPU project (potentially v10) beyond the

current 2nm TPU. Although the customer may adopt a COT (customer-owned
tooling) model with potential dual-foundry supply (TSMC and Samsung foundry),
we believe MediaTek could still enjoy strong revenue/earnings growth from 2029.
We expect MediaTek’s gross margin for the Google TPU to decline from c.40% in
2026, to around 30%-35% in 2029, but operating margin may stay at 25%-30%,
accretive to corporate operating margin at around 20%-25%. We therefore raise our

intermediate growth rate for MediaTek from 9% to 12%. If MediaTek also started to
provide a full-rack turnkey service in 2028, there could be more revenue upside.
Raise PT to NT$5,088: We estimate Google TPU to account for 38% and 63% of
MediaTek’s revenue in 2027 and 2028, making the stock one of the purest Google
TPU plays in Asia Tech. Our new PT of NT$5,088 implies 38x our 2027e EPS and 18x
our 2028e EPS. We believe the stock deserves a re-rating, given a strong TPU

pipeline with close to 50% revenue CAGR over 2025–28e. We believe the agentic AI
replacement cycle may also start in 2027 for high-end Android phones, driven by
Gemini Spark
– another option value on MediaTek.

### Page 2

Idea
2
Strong AI ASIC upside in 2028 from 2nm TPU
The 2nm TPU will adopt dual sourcing – both TSMC CoWoS‑L and Intel EMIB
TSMC’s CoWoS (Chip-on-Wafer-on-Substrate) is the industry’s leading 2.5D packaging
solution, particularly for AI and HPC applications. Its key advantage lies in the use of a
high-density interposer or RDL layer, which enables extremely wide, low-latency
connections between logic dies and HBM stacks. This makes it well suited for bandwidth-

intensive workloads such as GPUs and AI accelerators.
However, these benefits come at a cost: CoWoS – particularly the silicon interposer/bridge
– is expensive, capacity-constrained, and subject to yield challenges, including potential
warpage issues at larger chip sizes. Cost and manufacturing complexity remain key trade-
offs. As a result, we see TSMC’s CoWoS roadmap supporting only up to ~9.7x reticle size,
which does not appear sufficient for future AI or HPC chip designs.

We expect MediaTek to secure a portion of CoWoS capacity for 2nm TPU to ensure
minimum volume production, but we believe it will use Intel’s (covered by Joseph Moore)
EMIB-T as the primary solution to reduce costs and scale volume in 2028, if execution
proceeds well.
Exhibit 1: TSMC's roadmap for interposer size
Source: Winway, TSMC
Exhibit 2: Intel's EMIB can easily support larger chips  with
more reticles  (>12x) if its supply chain execution proceeds well

Source: Intel
Margin trend and full‑rack system support. In response to our question on providing
full-rack support during its earnings call (referencing recent Broadcom-Google
collaboration), MediaTek indicated it is in discussions with the customer. Management
expects any full‑rack system design support to materialize after 2027.
On margin trends, management highlighted continued value addition to the customer
across both silicon and packaging design. As a result, ASIC should remain accretive to

### Page 3

Morgan Stanley Research 3
operating margins

Google 2nm TPU the major growth driver for MediaTek from 
2028 
On April 22, Google introduced TPU v8 at Google Cloud Next, featuring two purpose-built 
architectures for training and inference: TPU 8t and TPU 8i. Our recent supply chain 
checks across multiple companies on reticle size confirm that MediaTek’s 3nm TPU 8 
corresponds to TPU 8t. This supports our view that MediaTek’s TPU performance should 
be sufficient for AI training.
According to Google’s announcement, “both chips will be generally available later this 
year and can be used as part of Google’s AI Hypercompute,” in line with MediaTek’s 
guidance that AI ASIC will contribute ~US$2bn of revenue in 2026 (we now assume 450-
500k units at a US$4,500 ASP).
In addition, our recent checks suggest that ASP for the next-generation 2nm Humufish 
TPU, supported by MediaTek, could exceed our prior expectations, driven by an increased 
number of main dies. For Humufish, checks across multiple sources indicate the number of 
main dies per chip will significantly exceed Zebrafish (one compute die with one I/O die). 
Combined with a SerDes upgrade and larger packaging size, we estimate ASP at US$12–
15k (we now model US$13k).
At the gross margin level, management indicated margins vary by project, which may also 
reflect potential full-rack system support. We model 2nm TPU gross margin at c.35% (vs. 
c.40% for 3nm TPU) given larger revenue scale, while we still expect operating margin at 
~20–25%, above the smartphone BU’s 15–20%.

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Android AI agent phones as an emerging trend from Google
I/O
Android AI agent phones – a trend to watch: Google is actively rolling out AI agents on
Android phones, starting with Galaxy S26 and Pixel 10 in early 2026. These agents can
perform multi-step tasks across apps – for example, ordering food, organizing notes, or
creating calendar events through direct interaction with app UIs. Over time, we expect
broader adoption across the Android ecosystem. Token consumption and cost remain key

constraints for agentic AI, and we believe on-device computing on edge devices such as
smartphones will help alleviate part of this cost burden.
Google I/O 2026 – Gemini evolves into an Agentic platform: At Google I/O 2026,
Google repositioned Gemini from a chatbot to a proactive AI “personal agent” capable of
autonomously handling tasks across apps and services. Google introduced several new
technologies, including Gemini Spark – a persistent assistant operating in the background

across Workspace tools – Gemini 3.5 Flash for faster real-time interaction, and Gemini
Omni for advanced multimodal capabilities spanning text, audio, images, and video. The
company also announced AI-driven changes to Google Search and deeper Gemini
integration across Android, YouTube, shopping, and future XR devices, signaling a broader
push to embed AI as a continuously available assistant that manages workflows and
automates user tasks. We believe the agentic AI replacement cycle could start in 2027

from high-end Android phones – another option value on MediaTek.
Exhibit 8: Gemini transforms into 'agentic' platform
Source: Google

Morgan Stanley Research 7
Raising price target from NT$2,988 to NT$5,088
We continue to derive our price target (our base case scenario value) from a residual
income model. The price target change reflects our EPS estimate revisions, which are
higher in outer years.  In addition to raising the intermediate growth rate to 12% (from 9%)
given the strong TPU pipeline, our other key valuation assumptions remain unchanged:
cost of equity at 9.2% (beta of 1.2, risk-free rate of 2%, and risk premium of 6%) and

terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Our bull and bear case scenario values also rise from  NT$4,100 and  NT$1,520  to NT
$6,988 and  NT$2,588, respectively.

M

8
Relative valuation
We view the P/E as relatively attractive – even after the share price rally – given strong AI
demand. Although MediaTek faces smartphone headwinds in 2026, we believe it is time to
look beyond 2026; we think this margin pressure is fully priced in, while TPU upside is not.
We believe the stock should trade more than +1SD above its historical average, supported
by strong TPU demand. Our new price target of NT$5,088 implies 38x 2027e EPS.

MediaTek receives US licenses for shipmentsto all Chinese smartphone brands. Emergingmarket demand recovers to trend-linegrowth. NT$5,088.00
38x 2027e EPS

Besides smartphones, AI ASICshould be the largest focus for the companynow. We expect a 40-50% revenue CAGRduring 2025-28. Non-smartphone businessdevelopment stays on track, including IoT,autos, enterprise networking chips, edge AIprojects and AI ASIC business.

Competitive pressures are worse thanexpected, with some market share loss,which hurts gross margin. Emerging marketsmartphone growth stalls. Chinesesmartphone chipset vendors show abreakthrough in 5G technologies or foundrysupply and start to take more market sharein mid-range 5G smartphone SoCs. Slownew business development in ASIC, IoT andautomotive, edge AI projects and AI ASICbusiness.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

## Document 076: 260526_ubs_MU

Source PDF: all report pdf/260526_ubs_MU.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-05-26
Pages: 28
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 26 May 2026
Global Research
Micron Technology Inc
LTAs Gain Traction; PT to $1,625 with EPS
Remaining Well >$100
Summary
With LTAs now firmly in place across most of the industry, we are again raising C2027-
2029 estimates and expect EPS to remain comfortably >$100 throughout the period,
with MU generating over $400B in FCF across the same timeframe. We believe the
market will start to put a more “normal” multiple on the stock and  MU will continue to

re-rate higher as more details emerge about the structural changes AI has driven to the
entire memory complex. Our supply chain work on Long Term Agreements (LTAs) across
the memory industry suggests that up to 30% of DDR volumes industry-wide will be
soon locked in at pricing that is just slightly below current levels and these agreements
will allow MU to trade some near-term revenue for demand visibility and a smoother
earnings profile. Consequently, we are raising EPS across C27/28/29E to $155/$167/

$117 (vs. $133/$122/$77 prior) - and considering that investors typically reward stocks
for durability and visibility, we see MU’s EPS remaining >$100 through C2029E as
testament to the sort of lasting, structural change that should support a shift toward a
broader semi multiple. Net, we lift our PT from $535 to $1,625, now based on ~15x
NTM P/E (vs. prior SoTP) on C2029E EPS of $117 (1-yr discounted), and reiterate Buy.
Long Term Agreements (LTAs) - Details and Rationale

In contrast to prior periods where offtake agreements were simply volume based, these
new ‘enhanced’ LTAs now incorporate longer durations, fixed volume commitments and
- most importantly - a partially fixed pricing framework. It is our understanding that
these 'enhanced' LTAs now span over a ~3-5 year period in either a '2+3' structure (two
years of fixed terms, three years variable), or a '3+2' format. In DRAM, we think 20-30%
of industry C2027 DDR bit shipments will now be covered under 'enhanced' LTAs. By

company, we assume 18% SK Hynix, 20% MU, and 30% Samsung – this is contracted
volume with fixed pricing, while pricing will still ‘float’ for the remainder of bits. At this
point, we  believe hyperscalers alone to have currently secured ~60-70% of the industry
Server DDR5 volumes under 'enhanced' LTAs - in other words, guaranteeing MU and
others offtake for this volume. In NAND, a similar pricing structure may be emerging,
with roughly 20% of volumes potentially moving to fixed-volume agreements but

Samsung, SK Hynix, and likely MU seem less willing to commit new cleanroom capacity
to NAND with the same level of certainty as DRAM. In assessing the rationale for
'enhanced' LTAs, we see two primary benefits for memory suppliers: (1) smoother
earnings/revenue profile and higher cross-cycle ROIC, and (2) improved visibility into
now-committed customer demand. On cyclicality, incorporating LTAs into our MU
model suggests these agreements should reduce the magnitude of DDR peak-to-trough

pricing swings by roughly 2x compared to a scenario with no committed volumes or
pricing structure. Even more important, this scenario yields MU EPS of >$100 even out in
C2029E when we do believe DRAM pricing will undergo a fairly significant correction. In
other words, we think MU still earns >$100 even if pricing for the floating portion of

DDR declines by ~50%. Conversely, from the hyperscaler perspective, the rationale for

Summary of Model Changes

We now expect the DRAM industry to remain undersupplied until at least C2Q28 (vs.
4Q27 prior), and NAND undersupply to last until 4Q27 (was 3Q27). An additional driver
of upside vs. our prior model stems from higher HBM ASP ($/GB) assumptions -
something we highlighted already early in April - as MU/SK/Samsung intend to rebuild a
premium for HBM pricing into C2027, leading us to now model MU HBM ASP up
~50% Y/Y (vs. +35% Y/Y prior) on unchanged MU HBM bit shipment assumptions of

7.78B Gb for C2026 and 12.05B Gb for C2027. Into CQ3:26 our industry model now
also considers the impact of LTAs - as primarily reflected in the magnitude of the
quarterly DDR (up ~8% Q/Q) and NAND (up ~9% Q/Q) industry contract pricing
increase. This implies Q/Q pricing growth now normalizing back toward the low SD%
range after  three, consecutive quarters of pricing increases averaging >50%.
Valuation: PT to $1,625 (from $535)
We raise our price target from $535 to $1,625 based on a ~15x NTM P/E multiple

(noting we see no reason why MU should trade a whole lot differently than NVDA in
terms of P/E), which is in line with the 3-yr average, and applied to our ~$117 C2029E
EPS and discounted back to C2028E using a COE of ~12%. Importantly, we anchor on
C2029E EPS as we believe it best reflects MU’s through-cycle earnings power under LTAs
because, by that point, our model assumes a moderate memory downcycle, albeit with
LTAs buoying the company’s earnings base.

### Page 12

Micron Technology Inc26 May 2026 ab 12
Micron Technology Inc UBS Research
ValuatioN
We raise our price target from $535 to $1,625. In doing so, we shift our valuation
framework to a NTM P/E methodology from a prior SoTP approach. We believe this
change is warranted as Long Term Agreements (LTAs) are fundamentally changing the
earnings profile of Micron, driving a structural re-rating of the shares. Specifically, while
MU has historically traded as a hyper-cyclical name, we now see LTAs effectively

anchoring demand and pricing, which should materially reduce earnings volatility and
enhance visibility. As a result, we believe MU increasingly merits a valuation in line with
broader Semiconductor peers, rather than at a discounted, hyper cycle-driven multiple.
Net, our price target is based on a ~15x NTM P/E multiple (vs. our prior ~5x NTM P/E
multiple implied by our SoTP valuation), applied to our ~$117 C2029E EPS and
discounted back to C2028E using a COE of ~11.8%. Importantly, we anchor on C2029E

EPS as we believe it best reflects MU’s through-cycle earnings power under LTAs as,  by
that point, our model assumes a moderate memory downcycle, albeit with LTAs buoying
the company’s earnings base.

### Page 14

Micron Technology Inc26 May 2026 ab 14
Micron Technology Inc UBS Research
Upside/Downside Spectrum
Upside/Downside Spectrum
Source: UBS estimates
UPSIDE ($1,850): We assume 29E HBM ASP to decrease by -6% and HBM bit to grow by
18% Y/Y. Excluding HBM,   we see DRAM ASP to go down by -42% while DRAM bit to
grow by 2% Y/Y. For NAND, we assume C29E NAND ASP to decrease by  -52%  and
NAND bit to increase by 13% Y/Y. By applying a ~16.0x P/E multiple, resulting in an

upside case of $1,850.
BASE ($1,625): We assume 29E HBM ASP to increase by 5% and HBM bit to grow by
30% Y/Y. Excluding HBM,   we see DRAM ASP to decrease  by -45% while DRAM bit to
grow by 14% Y/Y. For NAND, we expect C29E ASP to decrease by -56% and NAND bit to
grow by 9% Y/Y. By applying a ~15.0x P/E multiple, resulting in a base case of $1,625.
DOWNSIDE ($250): We assume 29E HBM ASP to decrease by -43% and HBM bit to
decline by -33% Y/Y. Excluding HBM,   we see DRAM ASP to down by -70% while DRAM

bit to decrease by -49% Y/Y. For NAND, we assume C29E ASP to decrease by  -77% and
NAND   bit to decrease by -45% Y/Y. By applying a ~11.0x P/E multiple, resulting in a
downside case of $250.

Company Description
Micron Technology Inc. designs and manufactures semiconductor memory and storage
solutions. Micron operates in four segments. The Compute and Networking Business Unit
serves the client, cloud server, enterprise, graphics, and networking markets. The Mobile

Business Unit addresses the needs of  the mobile handset and tablet markets. The Storage
business unit sells into cloud, enterprise and client storage markets. The Embedded Business
Unit sells memory to automotive, industrial, and consumer customers. Micron was founded in
1978 and is headquartered in Boise, ID.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Micron Technology Inc (MU): to arrive at our PT we use a valuation method solely based on
NTM P/E. Memory has been fairly correlated to global GDP . Therefore from a macro

perspective there could be potential downside to our PT should economic conditions worsen
and leading to demand destruction. Should ASP declines be more severe than our
expectations, there could be more downside in the stock. Scaling both DRAM and NAND is
highly complex and risks on the Technology front abound. There could be further downside
should technology migration be slower than we expect. On the upside, while we expect ASPs
to decline on account of incremental supply addition, any delays to the timelines could

moderate the growth of supply and create an upward bias to ASPs and MU. Other risks
include a incremental reliance of AI on memory driving up content in Data Center servers and
devices (PCs, SPs, etc).

Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

新興 (2605 TT)  Buy

油輪租金提升有利營運顯著改善，投資建議調升至 Buy。  
投資建議： 考量油輪租金提升有利營運顯著改善，荷莫茲海峽
若開放有望提升原油庫存回補需求，投資建議調升至 Buy ，目
標價 36 元。  
公司船隊規劃 : 新興於 1H26 陸續處分 1 艘 Capesize 船舶 (青山
輪 )及 1 艘 VLCC( 高登輪 )，目前旗下船舶包括 6 艘 Capesize 船
舶、 4 艘 Kamsarmax 型、 1 艘 VLOC 船舶、 2 艘 VLCC ；此外，
公司已購入一艘二手成品油輪，預計於 06~07/2026 交船，屆時
船隊艘數將達 14 艘。  
荷莫茲海峽若開放有利原油庫存回補需求回升，支撐油輪運價
走勢 :油輪方面，美伊戰爭爆發前，市場出現原油搶運需求，加
上戰爭期間荷姆茲海峽封鎖，致使油輪租金急速攀高； 2Q26 以
來隨美伊逐漸展開停火協議談判，油輪租金出現回落，然仍顯
著高於戰前的租金水準。近日美伊停火協議有望延長 30-60 天
協商，荷莫茲海峽有望於此段期間開放，有利原油庫存回補需
求，支撐油輪運價走勢。  
預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 5.23 元 :新興 1Q26 單季稅後獲利 3.87 億
元， YoY+2828.5% ，稅後 EPS 0.66 元。營運較 1Q25 大幅改善
主因在於各類船型運價皆有所調漲，其中以油輪部門獲利貢獻
提升幅度最大，因中東戰事導致荷姆茲海峽無法通行、進而推
升油輪租金價格上漲所致 。 2Q26 油輪共營租金價格有望維持 10
萬美元 /日以上的水準，另一艘油輪進塢檢修後於 06/2026 下旬
開始將執行 10-12 萬美元 /日的合約、為期 9 個月，另 2Q26 下
旬明和輪換約後租金較舊約上漲；高登輪及青山輪處分後營收
及本業獲利貢獻減少，然業外有望認列處分利益共計 4,200 萬
美元 (稅前 EPS 貢獻約 2.25 元 )，預估新興單季稅後獲利 15.5 億
元，稅後 EPS 2.65 元。展望 2H26 ，海岬型船舶進入需求旺季帶
動換約租金回溫，以及 3 艘船舶將陸續進塢維修，加上成品油
輪加入營運未營運帶來正向貢獻，預估 3Q26 及 4Q26 稅後 EPS
分別為 0.94 元及 0.98 元。整體而言，預估新興 2026 年稅後獲
利 30.63 億元， YoY+261.49% ，稅後 EPS 5.23 元。

2 群益投顧

公司 ESG 政策：  
(一 )企業對於因應氣候變遷或溫室氣體管理之策略  
新興航運持續推動低碳策略，鼓勵同仁響應節能減碳措施，積極替換高耗能
設備，持續精進低碳行動方案措施，達到降低能耗與減少溫室氣體排放之目
標。  1. 營業據點響應低碳活動  2. 船隊響應低碳活動  3. 置換低耗能 /節能設
備、新設備或油料  4. 持續改善機制。  
(二 )企業溫室氣體排放量減量目標  
本公司總部及船隊之溫室氣體排放量減量目標分列如下 : 目  標
2022~2025( 短期 ) 2026~2030( 中期 ) 2031~2050( 長期 ) 總部 (基準年 2021) 排
放量減少 40% 排放量減少 90% 淨零碳排  船隊 (基準年 2008) 排放量減少
9% 排放量減少 40% 排放量減少 70% 。  
(三 )企業溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫  
1、辦公室與船隊陸續規劃進行溫室氣體盤查與查證。  2 、規劃購買綠電；
無法減少的溫室氣體，規劃購買碳權。  3 、因應策略， IOM 船隊碳排放量，
申報及管控要求，對於營運成本將提升，同時船隊逐步優化與增添船舶減碳
設備、更換再生油料，甚或購買新船舶，以降低溫室氣體排放，管控營運成
本。  4 、天氣公司，合作與分享風險預警、情報監測與溝通工作。  5 、評估
合約、保險，及強化補償機制。  6 、未來嘗試納入氣候科學與長期預測，提
升氣候事件的適應能力。  
(四 )企業產品或服務帶給客戶或消費者之減碳效果  
我們將透過高標準的船舶維護作業，以負責任的態度，適當地運用節能科技
或其他科技與最佳實務，於持續提供我們的客戶安全與及時的遠洋運輸服
務的過程中，兼顧防止海洋 染與降低溫室氣體排放的社會使命。同時，我
們將會透過購置新船或現成二手船，提升船隊的運作效率，期能永續地為我
們的利害關係人創造最佳利益。  
新興船隊：  
新興於 1H26 陸續處分 1 艘 Capesize 船舶 (青山輪 )及 1 艘 VLCC( 高登輪 )，
目前旗下船舶包括 6 艘 Capesize 船舶、 4 艘 Kamsarmax 型、 1 艘 VLOC 船
舶、 2 艘 VLCC ；此外，公司已購入一艘二手成品油輪，預計於 06~07/2026
交船，屆時船隊艘數將達 14 艘。  
新購入的成品油輪於 2019 年建造完成，目前附有定期租約，日租金 18,000
美元，公司接船後預計租約仍將持續 12 個月。公司首次經營成品油輪，主
因看好相關市場需求波動風險較小，亦可增加油輪收益來源之多元性。

4 群益投顧 
部分油輪逐漸通行荷姆茲海峽後，油輪租金出現回落，然仍高於戰前的租金
水準。近日美伊停火協議有望延長 30-60 天協商，荷莫茲海峽有望於此段期
間開放，有利原油庫存回補需求，支撐油輪運價走勢。  
圖二、中國進口原油運價指數

資料來源：群益投顧彙整  
新興 2025 年稅後 EPS 1.45 元：  
公司 2025 年稅後獲利 8.47 億元， YoY-43.47% ，稅後 EPS 1.45 元。獲利衰
退主因包括海岬型及卡薩姆型船舶租金下滑，加上 2024 年認列 2 艘船舶處
分利益、基期墊高所致。  
處分船舶利益貢獻下，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 5.23 元：  
1Q26 單季稅後獲利 3.87 億元， YoY+2828.5% ，稅後 EPS 0.66 元。營運較
1Q25 大幅改善主因在於各類船型運價皆有所調漲，其中以油輪部門獲利貢
獻提升幅度最大，因中東戰事導致荷姆茲海峽無法通行、進而推升油輪租金
價格上漲所致。  
2Q26 油輪共營租金價格有望維持 10 萬美元 /日以上的水準，另一艘油輪進
塢檢修後於 06/2026 下旬開始將執行 10-12 萬美元 /日的合約、為期 9 個月，
另 2Q26 下旬明和輪換約後租金較舊約上漲；高登輪及青山輪處分後營收及
本業獲利貢獻減少，然業外有望認列處分利益共計 4,200 萬美元 (稅前 EPS
貢獻約 2.25 元 )，預估新興單季稅後獲利 15.5 億元，稅後 EPS 2.65 元。  
展望 2H26 ，海岬型船舶進入需求旺季帶動換約租金回溫，以及 3 艘船舶將
陸續進塢維修，加上成品油輪加入營運未營運帶來正向貢獻，預估 3Q26 及
4Q26 稅後 EPS 分別為 0.94 元及 0.98 元。  
整體而言，預估新興 2026 年稅後獲利 30.63 億元， YoY+261.49% ，稅後 EPS
5.23 元。

  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

2Q26 預期 AEC 高階產品出貨逐步恢復，估 EPS 季增 35.8% 
貿聯 1Q26 毛利率季減 3.2ppts 至 28.8%，低於市場預期 3.9ppts，主因 2-3
月高階 AEC 出貨遞延，低階產品大幅提升，EPS 達 11.66 元，低於市場預
期 19.7%。展望 2Q26，本中心預期 AEC 出貨遞延僅屬客戶短期拉貨時程調
整，隨高階產品出貨恢復，HPC 毛利率有望逐步改善，公司預估 2026 年毛
利率維持於 31–33% 區間。本中心預估貿聯 2Q26 營收季增 8.7%至 226.7
億；毛利率估季增 3.3ppts 至 32.1%，較前次下調 1.8 個 ppts 主因低階產品
比重高於預期；EPS 15.83 元，較前次下調 7.9%，主因下修毛利率預估。 
HPC 持續受惠 Power 規格升級；工業與車用成長優於預期 
展望 2H26，隨機櫃功耗將由 GB200/300 的 120-150 kW 提升至 200kW 以
上，預估 VR200 機櫃產值可較 GB 系列提升 2-3 倍，而 2027-2028 年進入
將改採獨立 Power rack ，預估 Power whip 需求數量將提升 2-3 倍，預估貿
聯 HPC 業務 2026/2027 年營收分別年增 72%/64%。非 HPC 業務方面，工
業營收表現優於預期，成長來自工廠自動化復甦及半導體設備拉貨強勁，估
工業 2026/2027 年營收各分別年增 20%，較前次的 8%上調；車用同樣看見
復甦，動能來自 Tesla 電動車 BMS 線束，估車用 2026/2027 年營收分別年
增 9%/10%，較前次的年持平上調。 
重申貿聯買進評等，目標價 2,800 元 
此次下修貿聯 2026 年 EPS 6% ，主因 AEC 高階產品比重低於預期，下調毛
利率預估；上調 2027 年 EPS 7% ，主因工業及車用優於預期，上調營收預
估。我們認為貿聯股價已反映 1Q26 產品組合不利因素，並看好高階 AEC
拉貨動能將回升，隨 GPU 密度提升帶動機櫃短距互連需求，AEC 滲透率仍
具高成長空間，並逐步往 800G/1.6T 升級長期趨勢不變，因此維持貿聯買進
評等，以 2027 年 EPS 110.21 元、25 倍本益比，推得目標價 2,800 元。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
1Q26 EPS 低於元大/市場預期 19.1%/19.7%，主因毛利率不如預期；
2Q26 AEC 高階產品出貨逐步恢復，估 EPS 季增 35.8%至 15.83 元。 
⧫  
持續看好 HPC 受惠 Power 規格升級，隨 AEC 高階產品出貨恢復，毛
利率將逐步改善；工業與車用成長優於預期，上調 2027 年 EPS 7%。 
⧫  
AEC 滲透率仍具高成長空間，並逐步往 800G/1.6T 升級長期趨勢未
變，維持買進評等，目標價 2,800 元 (2027F EPS 110.21 元 x25PE)。

HPC 持續受惠 Power 規格升級；工業與車用成長優於預期 
貿聯的 Power 線束於 AWS、Microsoft 與 Nvidia GB/VR 等平台皆為主要供應商，展望 2H26，隨
機櫃功耗將由 GB200/300 的 120-150 kW 提升至 VR 系列的 200kW 以上，看好電源規格升級帶動
連接器產值提升，包括 1)Power Whip 預期由 60A 升級至 100A；2)Busbar 由 1400A 升級至
5000A，預估 VR200 機櫃產值可較 GB 系列提升 2-3 倍。而 2027-2028 年進入 Rubin Ultra 將改採
獨立 Power rack ，電壓升至 800V HVDC ，預估 Power whip 需求數量將提升 2-3 倍，預估貿聯
HPC 業務 2026/2027 年營收分別年增 72%/64%。  
非 HPC 業務方面，工業業務營收表現優於預期， 1Q26 呈現雙位數增長，主要來自工廠自動化復
甦及半導體設備拉貨強勁，估工業 2026/2027 年營收各分別年增 20%，較前次的 8%上調。車用業
務同樣看見復甦，動能來自 Tesla 電動車 BMS 線束，估車用 2026/2027 年營收分別年增 9%/10%，
較前次的年持平上調。電器業務仍未見明顯回升，預期隨 2H26 併購墨西哥家電後有望擴大營運規
模，估電器 2026/2027 年營收分別年減 3%/年增 11%。

2028 年 CPO 將進入大規模商業化時程，貿聯將切入光學解決方案 
貿聯 2026-2027 年成長動能來自 1)AEC 滲透率持續提升，估貿聯今明年 AEC 業務年增率>50%；
2)Power Whip/Busbar 產品隨電源規格升級帶動產值提升。2028 年預期 CPO 將進入大規模商業化
時程，CPO 架構下光連接元件之重要性顯著提升，貿聯積極布局光通訊領域，2026 年 1 月正式收
購新富生光電 XFS，產品包括光纖跳線、 MPO/MTP 高密度連接器與多芯光纖組件。同時亦與
SENKO 及 ficonTEC 攜手合作 FAU 關鍵模組，使其產品組合從既有的高速線材，有望進一步延伸
至 MPO 連接、Fiber Assembly 及光纖重排模組 shuffle box 等關鍵元件，貿聯預期 3Q26 於台南設
立光通產線，4Q26 有望初步貢獻，主要放量時間點為 2028 年。 
圖 4：新富生光電 XFS MPO Connector 圖 5：新富生光電 XFS 光纖配線產品圖

資料來源：GTC、元大投顧整理 資料來源：GTC、元大投顧整理 
預期 3Q26 於台南設立光通產線，看好與康寧合作之機會 
在 CPO 架構下，光模組被拆分為光引擎（OE）、光連接元件（如 FAU、MPO、Shuffle Box ）與
封裝等子系統，其中光連接元件的重要性顯著提升，成本占比可達三成以上，且為良率與性能關鍵，
帶動相關供應鏈商機浮現。 
其中 Shuffle Box 將千上萬條光纖進行高密度重新分配與匯聚，為 AI 資料中心關鍵的被動光學元件。
根據供應鏈調查，shuffle box ASP 估達 4000-6000 美金，一台 switch 約搭配 2 個 shuffle box，而
以 2027 年 scale out CPO 滲透率 12%假設，約隱含 13 萬個 shuffle box 需求，2027 年市場規模估
計達 200 億台幣，而隨 2028 年 CPO 進入大規模導入階段，預計市場規模將進一步倍增，本中心
看好 Shuffle Box 相關商機，具備 Shuffle Box 技術之台廠包含波若威及貿聯，看好光通題材有望帶
動貿聯評價走升。 
➢ 波若威  : 持續投入光纖配 線盒、 800G/1.6T 光纖套 件、客製化 Jumper 產品以及 MMC 
Jumper 等高密度光互連產品，估 2H26 進入量產階段。 
➢ 貿聯 : 收購新富生光電 XFS 後，亦同樣具備 Shuffle Box 生產能力，不排除與康寧合作之機
會，預期 3Q26 於台南設立光通產線，4Q26 有望初步貢獻，主要放量時間點為 2028 年。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電腦周邊 2026 年 5 月 26 日 BizLink 第5 頁，共 17頁
產業概況
銅纜仍為 Scale-Up 短距連線最具效益的方案
資料中心伺服器架構可分為 Scale-Up 垂直擴展與 Scale-Out 水平擴展兩種模式。Scale-Up 透過單
機增加 CPU/GPU 及儲存容量，對機櫃內短距離高速線材需求顯著；Scale-Out 則透過增加節點強
調系統可擴展性，對機櫃間/資料中心中長距離光互連需求強勁。雖 CPO 與矽光子技術持續推進，
然而在 AI 資料中心的 Scale-Up 場景中，相較光學互連，銅纜可省略光電轉換，在延遲、功耗與成
本方面具顯著優勢。整體而言，光通訊已在 Scale-Out 場景取得主導地位，然而 Scale-Up 仍為銅
纜主導，市場普遍預期光學方案大規模導入時點將落在 2028 年之後，較市場預期延後約 0.5–1 年。
圖 7：Scale-Up 與 Scale-Out 架構比較
Scale-Up Scale-Out

連接範圍 機櫃內 機櫃間/資料中心
核心概念 強化單一節點效能 擴散整理系統規模
延遲要求 極低 相對較高
優勢 避免光電轉換延遲與功耗 克服長距離傳輸損耗
主要互連技術 NVLink / UALink Ethernet / InfiniBand
傳輸媒介 銅為主(PCB/AEC) 光為主(Optical)
供應鏈受惠 高階 PCB / 連接器 光模組 / 交換器
資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理
AEC 為 3-7 公尺中短距離主流，滲透率仍具高成長空間
高速互連技術依應用距離與功耗需求，可分為 DAC 被動銅纜、AEC 主動式電纜、AOC 主動光纜。
傳統 DAC 雖具備低成本、低功耗與低延遲優勢，但在單通道速率提升至 100G/200G 後，其有效傳
輸距離僅剩 1–2 公尺，應用場景快速受限。在此背景下，AEC 透過導入 Retimer 晶片，實現訊號
重整與放大，使傳輸距離可延伸至 5–7 公尺。
以 Credo 為例，其 AEC 解決方案已可在 100G 單通道達 7 公尺傳輸距離，並在 800G 世代維持穩

定效能，而隨著 AEC 能夠跨越多個機架，系統設計不再受限於傳輸距離，進一步降低光模組導入
需求。400G 世代中 DAC 於短距離滲透率約 70–80%，隨傳輸速率提升至 800G，訊號衰減限制將
使其滲透率下降至 50%以下，而 AEC 因可內建 Retimer，延伸傳輸距離並改善訊號完整性，預估
自 800G 滲透率由低雙位數快速提升至中高雙位數，成為中短距離主流方案，具備 Retimer 與高速
互連技術能力的廠商 Credo 為主要受惠者，而其 AEC 獨家代工廠商貿聯亦同步受益，預估貿聯今
明年 AEC 業務年增率>50%。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電腦周邊 2026 年 5 月 26 日 BizLink 第6 頁，共 17頁  
GPU 密度提升帶動機櫃短距互連需求，銅纜優勢持續強化 
資料中心互連設計由 Hopper 世代以約 20 公尺光模組連接為主，演進至 Blackwell NVL72 採用約 
7 公尺 AEC，並進一步於 Rubin NVL144 導入 5 公尺 1.6T AEC，並採「4 個 GPU 機架 + 1 個網
路機架」架構，透過 72 條 AEC 實現後端互連、18 條用於前端連接； Rubin NVL576 則進一步提
升整合度，採「1 個 GPU 機架 + 1 個網路機架」高密度部署，將連接距離縮短至約 1.5–2 公尺，
單機架配置達 576 顆 GPU、功耗約 600kW，並採用 576 條 1.6T AEC 進行高速連接，有效提升布
線效率與空間利用率。 
整體而言，隨著機架內部連接距離持續縮短，銅纜在低延遲、高可靠性與功耗效率上的優勢得以進
一步放大，AEC 仍為主流互連方案，並隨 GPU 密度提升帶動用量與規格同步升級。 
圖 8：Rubin NVL144 透過 72 條 AEC 實現後端互連、18 條用於前端連接

資料來源：CREDO、元大投顧整理

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電腦周邊 2026 年 5 月 26 日 BizLink 第7 頁，共 17頁  
傳輸連接線 AEC/AOC/ACC/DAC 孰優？ 
資料中心中短距離互聯主要包括三種場景：伺服器與交換器互聯、記憶體與交換器互聯、交換器與
交換器互聯，其中線纜連接方案可分為 1) 主動式光纖電纜(Active Optical Cable ；AOC)；2) 直連
銅纜(Direct Attach Cable ；DAC)；3) 主動式電纜(Active Electrical Cable ；AEC)/有源銅纜(Active 
Copper Cable；ACC)。 
1) 主動式光纖電纜 AOC：由兩組光收發模組與一條光纖跳線組成，兩端有光收發晶片將訊號轉
為光訊號，並通過光纖進行訊號傳輸，具有重量輕、傳輸距離遠、對電磁輻射敏感低等優點，
但內部裝置了光收發模組，因此價格較高。 
2) 直連銅纜 DAC：由銅線組成，不需要光電轉換晶片，因此價格較低，但受銅物理性能限制使
DAC 傳輸距離較短，且傳輸距離隨帶寬增加而下降，當傳輸速率從 400G 向 800G 升級，DAC
傳輸距離從 3m 縮短至 2m。 
3) 主動式電纜 AEC/有源銅纜 ACC：相較 DAC 電纜兩端各自多了內置晶片，在短距離傳輸具備
低成本、低能耗等優勢，其中 ACC 兩端放置 Redriver、AEC 兩端放置 Retimer。Redriver 相
當於線性放大器，無法清除或降低電纜內的噪音，因此不會重新調節訊號，反而放大訊號與噪
音；Retimer 中包含了 CDR (時鐘數據恢復；Clock Data Recovery)與 DFE (決策反饋均衡器；
Decision Feedback Equalizer) ，可將訊號重新進行調節，消除噪音並放大訊號，實現更清晰的
數據傳輸，因此單價較 Redriver 更高。 
圖 10：Redriver (左)單純將線路內訊號一併放大，Retimer (右)對訊號進行調節並放大

資料來源：元大投顧整理

 AOC DAC AEC 
400G 傳輸距離 支持長距傳輸 <3m <7m 
800G 傳輸距離 支持長距傳輸 <2m <7m 
功耗 高，400G 為 AEC 兩倍以上 低，400G 為 AEC 的 1/4 中 
成本 高，400G 為 AEC 兩倍左右 低，400G 為 AEC 的一半 中 
佔用空間 較小 大，400G 為 AEC 的 4 倍 小

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理 
以上三種線纜連接方案各有所長，適用於不同場景，AOC 適用於長距離、大帶寬的互聯，DAC 則
適用於短距離，而 AEC 則是較具性價比的選擇方案，可以達 7m 距離、較 AOC 功耗低 50%、體
積較 DAC 低 75%、成本為 AOC 的一半。 
根據 LightCounting 數據顯示，AOC/DAC/AEC 2024 -2029 年市場規模將分別 CAGR+32%/+18% 
/+43%，合計規模將由 2024 年的 18 億元成長至 67 億美元。

獲利調整與股票評價
重申貿聯買進評等，目標價 2,800 元
整體而言，預估貿聯 2026/2027 年營收分別年增 36%/43%至 966 億元/1,385 億元，其中 HPC 占
比分別達 49%/56%；毛利率短期負面因素主要受 AEC 產品組合影響，預期 2H26 隨客戶拉貨動能
回穩後改善，毛利率估 32.1%/34.7%，EPS 估 67.48 元/110.21 元，分別年增 44.9%/63.3%。
此次下修貿聯 2026 年 EPS 6.3% ，主因客戶拉貨時程調整，AEC 高階產品比重下降，下調毛利率
預估；上調 2027 年 EPS 7.1%，主因工業及車用復甦優於預期，上調營收預估。我們認為貿聯股
價已反映 1Q26 產品組合不利因素，並看好 2H26 高階 AEC 拉貨動能回升，隨 GPU 密度提升帶動
機櫃短距互連需求，銅纜優勢持續強化，AEC 滲透率仍具高成長空間，並逐步往 800G/1.6T 升級

長期趨勢未變，因此維持貿聯買進評等，以 2027 年 EPS 1 10.21 元給予 25 倍本益比，推得目標價
2,800 元 (維持相同本益比評價)。

公司簡介  
貿聯-KY 主要提供關鍵元件、線束及電纜線予多元產業，產品及服務擴及資訊科技基礎設施、用戶端周邊設備、光通訊、
電信網絡、電器、醫療保健、工廠自動化、機械與感測、汽車產業、軌道車輛、船舶暨海洋產業、工業產業、太陽能等。
產品的主要原物料為線材、連接器、端子等，1Q26 營收組成工業 34%、電器 8%、車用 11%、資訊科技與數據傳輸 46%、
其他 1%。

### Page 14

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 貿聯-KY 整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於中風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排行在領先的位置，
亦於電子設備業的公司中排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 貿聯-KY 的整體曝險中等，略優於電子設備業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的 ESG 議題包含營
運方面的商業道德、產品治理與人力資本等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：貿聯-KY 在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力強。主因公司的揭露程度極佳，
表明對投資者及公眾的強烈責任感，同時公司於 ESG 亦有相關舉措，但尚不能藉此管理所有風險。儘管如此， 貿聯-KY
並未涉及任何與 ESG 相關的重大爭議。

材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
旭隼科技股成立於 2008 年，並於 2014 年掛牌上市，資本額 8.77
億元，主要從事不斷電供應系統 （ Uninterruptible Power Supply ，
簡稱 UPS ）、逆變器 (Inverter) 及太陽能產品之專業設計、研發、
生產和銷售。旭隼客戶群遍及 100 多個國家， UPS 產品在市場擁
有高度佔有率。旭隼 100% 執行 OEM/ODM 設計、研發、生產代
工方針，不開發自有品牌，不與客戶競爭市場，持續投入研發創
新，全力提供客戶高質量的產品，並協助客戶建立品牌效益，與
客戶建立長期的互信互利的關係。旭隼生產基地位於深圳總廠、
深圳內銷廠與廣東中山廠。旭隼 2024 年營收結構， On-line UPS 
33.2%、Off-line UPS 27.3% 、Inverter 31.6% 與 Accessory 7.9% 。

圖一、旭隼營運據點

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

二. 2026 年營運概況及展望

UPS 為發生停電或電壓不穩等電源問題時，可自動將電源切換為
內建電池，在短時間內繼續供應 PC 或週邊設備電力之裝置。電
源發生問題時， PC 或週邊設備可利用 UPS 供電期間進行關閉系
統作業，以便使系統安全停止運作。對於重度依賴著電子產品的
現今生活， UPS 的價值顯現在當供電情形異常如停電或電流不穩
時，仍可持續供應電子產品所需的穩定電流，故通常用於維持商
用電腦系統、通訊設備、精密儀器等電子產品的穩定運行，避免
操作中斷所帶來的損失。

### Page 3

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

3 
現行的鉛酸電池為主流的 UPS ，主要以電池、功率半導體、變壓
器、電源轉換器、電阻電容及其他相關零組件組成，其供電原理
是當市電正常時，機器會將市電的交流電 AC 轉換成直流電 DC，
並對電池充電。但當 UPS 偵測到市電電壓出現異常或是斷電時，
再將儲存於電池中的直流電轉換為交流電，供負載繼續使用，以
達到不斷電功能。一般對於 UPS 則依設計類別區分為在線式不斷
電系統（ On-Line UPS ）、離線式不斷電系統（ Off-Line UPS ）。

圖二、旭隼產品線

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

預估 2026 年 On-Line UPS 營收金額 79.6 億元， YoY+4.9%

在線式不斷電系統（ On-Line UPS ）主要優點在於能提供純淨交
流電力輸出，電力品質不良的情況能完全改善，但缺點在於結構
複雜、成本較高，價格也相對較高，故障可能性較離線式高，主
要應用於大型電信設備，醫療院所與企業伺服器等。

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保

預估 2026 年 Off-Line UPS 營收金額 52.5 億元， YoY-0.8%

離線式不斷電系統（ Off-Line UPS ）主要優於在於結構簡單、體
積小重量輕價格最低，但缺點在於對市場不經處理，電力品質不
良問題無法完全改善，轉換時間長，保護性最低，因此主要應用
於 PC 與個人應用為主。

公司研究報告

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保

Off-Line UPS 2025 年出貨量 522 萬台， YoY-12.01% ，貢獻旭隼
2025 年營收金額 52.92 億元， YoY-15.1% 。觀察 2025 年在全球
經濟存在不確定高，且 Off-Line UPS 主要以個人消費市場為主，
價格敏感度高，受到終端消費市場疲弱，導致出貨量與營收呈現
同步下滑。

預 Off-Line UPS 1Q26 出貨量 114 萬台， YoY-9.38% ，貢獻 1Q26
營收金額 12.40 億元， YoY-9.8% 。估 2026 年，在 Off-Line UPS
整體市場發展己達飽和，成長有限，加上下游客戶庫存水準偏高，
預估 2026 年全年 Off-Line UPS 出貨量 490~520 萬個，
YoY-6.08~-0..% ，預估 2026 年 Off-Line UPS 營收金額 52.50 億
元， YoY-0.8% 。

預估 2026 年 Inverter 營收金額 54.00 億元， YoY-8.9%

逆變器 (Inverter) 主要區分併網型 (On-Grid) 、離 線 型(Off-Grid) 與混
合型 (Hybrid) 。旭隼專注於離線型 (Off-Grid) 與混合型 (Hybrid) 的
Inverter 產品，客戶主要以非洲與南亞等缺乏電子基礎的國家；及
歐洲尖峰與離峰用電的電價差異大的地區，主要原因在於節省電
費會在離峰時間儲電，在尖峰時間使用。 Inverter 2025 年出貨量
78 萬台， YoY-7.96% ，貢獻旭隼 2025 年營收金額 59.31 億元，
YoY-17.6% 。觀察 2025 年，在新興市場需求疲弱，不利 Inverter
出貨，導致營收呈現下滑。

Inverter 1Q26 出貨量 12 萬台， YoY-18.92% ，貢獻 1Q26 營收金
額 7.93 億元， YoY-38.3% 。預期 2026 年，在美伊戰爭導致中東
地區出貨受到限制，整體出貨量將呈現衰退，但旭隼將推出特殊
用 Inverter 與高階商用 Inverter 等產品，以抵銷 Inverter 不利因
素，宏遠推估 2026 年全年 Inverter 出貨量 70~75 萬個，
YoY-9.68~-3.2% 預估 2026 年 Inverter 營收金額 54.00 億元，
YoY-8.9% 。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

預估 2026 年稅後盈餘 29.56 億元， YoY-16% ，稅後 EPS 33.7 元

旭隼 2026 年在 On-Line UPS 恢復成長 5%， 但受到 Off-Line UPS
與 Inverter 衰退影響，導致旭隼 2026 年營收呈現持平，獲利呈現
衰退。宏遠預估旭隼 2026 年營收金額 204.3 億元， YoY-0.45% ，
預估營業利益 37.26 億元， YoY-13.92% ，預估稅後盈餘 29.56 億
元， YoY-16.04% ，預估稅後 EPS 33.70 元。

營收走勢圖

研究員/張書明 brady.chang@uni-psg.com

研究部將倉和評等調升至強力買進 ， 係因 ： （1）研究部研判倉和將非
常有機會受惠 Tesla 29 億美元美國光伏在地製造的投資計劃，獲龐
大 、 穩定且高單價高毛利的海外訂單；（2）目前倉和為唯一光伏網版
非紅供應商 ， 非紅優勢使得美 、 印等海外市場營收佔比將逐年成長過
半；（3）中國市場佔比下降，擺脫中國過度競爭，營收獲利將穩定成
長有利評價提升 。綜上所述 ，儘管審慎看待 26 年中國光伏建置環境 ，
保守預估 26 年營收從 16.2 億元下修至 15.4 億元(+6.2%YoY)，但
公司仍受惠新產品與非中市場營收佔比提升，毛利率有望躍升至
38.8%（+5.6ppts），加上 3Q26 將有壞帳回沖，預估 26 年稅後 EPS
從 3.36 元上修至 5.88 元。27 年美國市場大單挹注後，稅後 EPS 有
望達 10.08 元，若 28-29 年美系客戶 100G 建置完畢，年獲利上修空
間極大。 考量公司營運低谷已過 ， 下檔風險有限 。 同時獲美系客戶龐
大 、 穩定且高單價高毛利的大單 ， 中國佔比下降 ， 將大幅改善公司營
收獲利成長與穩定性。以 27 年獲利給予歷史平均本益比 20 倍 PE，
先前目標價到價後調升至 200 元(2027 EPS*20PER)，後續仍有上修
空間。

重點摘要： 
1. 公司簡介 
倉和為全球精密網版領導廠商，提供研發、製作、銷售精密網
版與網印耗材， 依新到舊產品分為No Bridge 鋼板 （OF）（40%）、
不鏽鋼絲網版 （CF）（30%） 、傳統PI 網版 （SF）（30%） ，近九成
營收與太陽能電池出貨量連動 。 過去銷售重心集中於全球光伏
最大市場中國（近七成）與東南亞地區 ， 國內市場佔比僅3-4%。
主要生產基地包括台灣桃園龜山廠、中國成都廠與蘇州廠，產
能分布為台灣 40%（高端研發） 、中國60%（產線放量） 。主要
客戶包括：隆基綠能、Jinko Solar、韓華集團、天合光能等太
陽能廠商。倉和早期市佔曾高達 40%，主要競爭對手為 日本
Murakami、Sonocom。在昆山恒盛電子、昆山良品絲印刷器材、
昆山樂邦印刷器材設備等中國廠積極搶佔市場後 ， 目前市占約
20%，但整體倉和在提效、省漿、研發技術方面仍具備領先優
勢。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

圖一、 公司歷代產品介紹

資料來源：統一投顧整理

2. 倉和過往產品迭代帶動上行循環 
倉和在產品技術具產業領導地位。為追求銀漿細線、細高等電
池優化，過去每年均積極推出新產品，往往伴隨營收與獲利大
幅改善，形成產品換代循環。然而，中國為全球太陽能最大市
場，使得倉和過去在銷售市場無可避免地集中於中國，造就以
下產業困境：(1) 面對中國政府補助造成的過度競爭，往往造
成同業殺價競爭，導致倉和近年中國利潤下降。 (2) 中國專
利意識薄弱造成同業互相抄襲，使得倉和產品優勢迅速衰退。 
(3) 下游中國電池廠內卷，產業嚴重供過於求，使得下游客戶
良莠不齊，收款成為隱憂。綜上所述，使得倉和過去產品生命
週期縮短，影響公司長期獲利結構。

圖二、 倉和歷史循環

資料來源：財報狗、統一投顧整理

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

3. 光伏產業短期成長趨緩，有利長期產業競爭格局轉佳 
2025 年全球模組需求估計約 653–706 GW，成長逐漸趨緩。
中國與美國受政府均分別受多項政策 影響使得新增裝機 量更
趨保守，歐洲需求大致持平，印度則受本土化推進與內需拉動
逆勢成長。展望 2026 年恐面臨十年來首次衰退，模組需求預
估將下修至 529-624 GW ，主因來自傳統主力市場中國、美國
分別取消能源補貼，建置量持續收斂。越來越多國家也跟著開
始收緊新能源政策，使 2026 年出現短期低點。不過太陽能仍
為最重要的能源之一，除擁有最低廉的能源成本，隨著功率持
續推升、光伏加速， 以及新興市場拓展 、老舊項目汰換、AI 運
算中心能源建置等長期題材推進，需求的中長期韌性仍在，隨
之而來將進入高原緩增階段。面對短期衰退，產業有望汰弱留
強，進而改善嚴重內卷的競爭格局。

圖三、 全球太陽能模組需求趨勢，Unit: GW；%

資料來源：InfoLink、統一投顧整理

4. 光伏先進技術持續滲透，推動網版產品迭代 
過去太陽能電池主要為 P 型晶片 ， 近年逐漸被高效率與壽命的
N 型晶片取代。N 型又分為 TOPCon、HJT 和 IBC。工研院表示
TOPCon 技術與原有 P 型技術相容性高 ， 設備改建成本最低 ， 相
較於全新產線，升級僅需 20%成本。技術普及與規模經濟使生
產成本進一步下降，TOPCon 未來五年將為市場主流。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

圖四、 太陽能電池市佔率預估（2024 年）

資料來源：InfoLink、統一投顧整理

隨著 TOPCon、xBC、HJT 等先進技術滲透率提升，先進技術要
求線寬更細 、 截面更瘦高 、 壽命與尺寸穩定度更好 ， 因此即需
網版產品的迭代。倉和最新產品 No Bridge 鋼板（OF）將成
為高度選配甚至標配，提升新產品需求與滲透率提升。

5. 銀價等原物料上漲推升新網版需求 
近期工業用銀漿相較過往水準已上漲逾一倍，主因為 AI 資料
中心、能源轉型帶動的電力基建，使得銀漿需求增加。過去黃
金等貴金屬上漲也間接影響銀價跟漲 。 對於光伏產業將直接推
升太陽能電池銀漿、導電材料與部分電子耗材成本，壓縮下游
客戶毛利，進而加速產業導向少銀化、細線化、銅替代等技術
趨勢。多數客戶過去在選擇網版廠商已價格為導向，如今銀價
暴漲使得客戶改以省銀為優先。倉和新品No Bridge 鋼板較傳
統網版節省 30% 銀漿，公司將因此受惠新客戶轉單。

圖五、 白銀期貨價格

資料來源：MacroMicro、統一投顧整理

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

6. 倉和極高機率將獲 Tesla 自建在地製造的穩定大單 
過去馬斯克認為太陽能一向是全球最可靠、最便宜、目前能
源龐大缺口的最佳解方。尤其在 AI 資料中心與高科技廠房
高度擴張下，太陽能仍為 支撐美國未來電力需求的重要 來
源。2016 年 Tesla 即收購 Solar City，產品包括家用、商
用光伏與儲能系統，未來將從住宅、商用、公用事業延伸至
AI 基礎設施、低軌衛星、晶圓廠的整合布局。 
近年美國太陽能受到中美關稅影響導致部署成本大幅提升，
為了避免美中地緣風險、關稅問題與能源垂直整合自有化，
同時因應美國當地來自 AI 資料中心、高科技廠房的龐大用
電需求，1Q26 馬斯克宣布將斥資 29 億美元自建美國在地太
陽能垂直整合能力，計劃 在 28 年底前於美國建立 100GW 
的太陽能自製能力。 過往美國光伏年建置高峰約48GW，Tesla
計劃量體顯然為未來 AI 基建與高科技廠房佈局，使 Tesla 
成為美國最大的太陽能零組件製造商。投資內容主要向中國
太陽能設備商如蘇州 Maxwell、深圳 S.C New Energy 與 
Laplace 購買網版印刷產線、太陽能電池與面板的生產設備，
最早可於 3Q26 開始交付。 
針對長期穩定的網版供應商，倉和為耗材網版的領導廠商，
擁有高品質且提效、省漿的技術能力，研究部研判 Tesla 非
常有機會將優先尋求網版領導廠商倉和供應 100G 光伏產能
製程中的耗材 網版。目前競爭對手多來自 中國，面對 美國 
Section 301 逾 50% 關稅，加上 FEOC／PFE 等相關排中政
策，倉和高機率將獨享龐大、穩定且高單價高毛利的美國市
場。Tesla 27 年開始建置至 28 年底完成 100GW 產能，預期
27 年單一客戶營收佔比將近三成 。28 年營收再翻倍成長 ， 長
期將穩定貢獻年營收近 10 億元，營收佔比近四成，提升公
司獲利結構與穩定度。

7. 海外市場過半，中國佔比下降大幅改善獲利結構 
中國需求受到政策與併網速度影響 ，26 年光伏建置量恐衰退
2-4 成，使得 26 年 1、2 月營收接近歷史低點。不過受惠銀
漿上漲 ，26 年將新增中國太陽能電池大客戶 ， 有望使得26 年
中國市場仍維持成長。針對非中市場，美國、印度作為全球
前三大太陽能市場，約佔整體市場13-15%。近年推出多項政
策積極執行非紅供應鏈，公司有望在美國、印度等市場持續
提升市占率 。25 年底美國客戶認證完成 ，26 年起已開始逐季
放量。印度作為龐大且反中的潛在市場，也將成為公司繼中
國後的替代市場，目前印度 2-3 位客戶也已認證完畢將開
始出貨。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  6

面對龐大的潛在訂單，公司近期積極著手擴產計劃。26 年底
公司將遷入林口自建廠，同時保有原先龜山租賃廠，雙邊同
時生產有望提升 2-3 成產能 。 此外 ， 公司1Q26 赴印度訪查後
預計於印度租廠並在 1Q27 投產，同時在印度進行建廠於 28
年後投產，合計有望使產能翻倍，顯示公司對於海外市場前
景的信心。 
相較過度競爭的中國市場，非中市場為高單價、高毛利且穩
定訂單的客戶，來自中國同業的競爭將大幅下降。預期 26 年
非中市場將從過往三成提升至四成，27-28 年非中佔比更將
突破五成，使得公司營收獲利結構與穩定性將大幅改善，有
利評價提升。

圖六、 全球太陽能需求各國佔比（2024）

8. 營運低谷已過，2Q26 有望持續回溫  
25 年因中國過度競爭使得產業持續去化庫存 ，加上年初銀價
上漲、中國補貼取消政策均使得中國客戶維持觀望。1Q26 營
收 3.1 億元 （-0.5%QoQ，-27.2%YoY），已達最低基本拉貨量 ，
為營運低谷。 然而受惠新產品與非中客戶營收佔比提升，
1Q26 毛利率大幅躍升至 38.5%，同時公司積極降低費用，使
得 1Q26 稅後 EPS 0.88 元虧轉盈，大幅優於原先預期 0.10
元。 
倉和 4 月營收 1.3 億元 （+9%MoM，-19%YoY） ， 營收達成率 36% 
略優於預期，更加肯定營運低谷已過，預期5、6 月營收有望
維持 4 月水準，使得 2Q26 營收 3.9 億元（+25.7%QoQ，-
1.3%YoY），毛利率維持 1Q26 水準 ，預估稅後 EPS 持續成長至 
1.23 元。

### Page 7

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  7

9. 有望大啖美系客戶自建大單，營收獲利結構大幅昇華 
研究部研判公司高機率將受惠 Tesla 29 億美元美國在地
100G 光伏產能的投資計劃 ， 獲龐大 、 長期 、 穩定的海外訂單 。
美、印等海外市場營收佔比將過半，擺脫中國過度競爭的紅
海市場，營收獲利將大幅成長，有利評價提升。預期美系客
戶將於 4Q26 開始小量貢獻，27 年訂單量逐季提升，28 年底
100GW 建置完成後開始每年穩定供應完整產量。儘管審慎看
待 26 年中國光伏建置環境，保守預估 26 年營收從 16.2 億
元下修至 15.4 億元(+6.2%YoY)，但公司仍受惠新產品與非
中市場營收佔比提升 ， 毛利率有望躍升至38.8%（+5.6ppts）。
此外，25 年底一度將中國應收帳款打入壞帳，預計 3Q26 將
有壞帳回沖貢獻稅後 EPS 約 1.23 元，使得 26 年整體稅後 
EPS 預估將從 3.36 元上修至 5.88 元。27 年美國市場大單挹
注後，稅後 EPS 有望達 10.08 元，若 28-29 年美系客戶 100G
建置完畢，年獲利上修空間極大。訂單能見度明確，公司長
線成長可期。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

本 研 究 報 告 所 載 之 投 資 資 訊 ， 僅 提 供 客 戶 做 為 一 般 投 資 參 考 ， 並 非 針 對 特 定 對 象 提
供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之

## Document 119: 9921巨大 元富

本刊載之報告為元富投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬元富投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 009 號 
公司拜訪快報

巨大(9921) 
建議：維持中立 
結論：自行車市場需求仍待回溫

一、歐洲自行車市場逐漸復甦，中國市場修正幅度縮減 
巨大為全球最大的主要自行車廠，透過台灣、越南、荷
蘭 、 匈牙利等工廠 ， 調整最佳的生產配置 。 目前透過Giant
品牌銷售，與幫國際自行車品牌廠代工，並透過多品牌
策略，涵蓋高中低階款。1Q26 品牌與代工營收佔比分
別為 OBM 76%、OEM 24%，而地區營收佔比為歐洲
39%、中國 17%、北美 9%。 
根據自行車零件大廠 Shimano 最新預估 ， 整車恢復較零
組件緩慢，電動車調整速度較傳統自行車快，高階自行
車需求優於低階自行車。區域方面，歐洲市場已轉為正
成長，北美受關稅與通籐影響需求較不穩定，中國市場
持續調整但下滑幅度縮減。 
二、新車銷售的去化能否持續為觀察重點 
1Q26 營收 125.24 億元，QoQ 1.9%，YoY -25.7%，主
要為客戶代工訂單保守較去年同期下滑 54%。OB 與 OE
營收比重 2025 年的 61%、39%，而1Q26 比重為 OB 76%，
OE 24%增至 39%，在品牌比重提升下，雖然 1Q26 毛
利率仍略低於研究部預估，但較去年同期好轉 1.84%。
受到營收規模下降及 WRO 相關一次性支出費用，營導
致業利益虧損，單季 EPS -0.51 元。 
統計 1-4 月公司各地區品牌銷售，歐洲小幅成長，中國
市場下滑 25%，顯示產業持續調整。目前進入產業旺季
以及新車發表挹注下，預期 2Q26 營收 156.54 億元，
+25%QoQ，-0.6%YoY，費用在 WRO 影響至尾聲下，
推估 EPS 1.63 元 。 觀察公司存貨金額維持在相對低點，
不過在市場需求恢復較不如預期下，新車銷售的去化能
否持續將為庫存是否健康的觀察重點。 
三、美國海關扣留令至尾聲 
針對美國海關扣留令(WRO)，繼4Q25 提列 1 億多元費
用後，1Q26 再提列 8,000 萬元。目前公司補償移工完
成率達 80%以上，已進入最後結案階段，預期後續提列
費用將明顯減少 。 由於台灣出至美國的皆為高階自行車 ，
若此案告一段落，將有利營運改善。 
董事會通過買回 4,000 張庫藏股，買回期間約為 2026 
年 5 月 8 日至 7 月 7 日，買回價格區間為每股 60
～100 元。目的為維護股東權益，以及將買回股份轉讓
給員工。 
四、預期 2026 年 EPS 3.18 元 
展望 2026 年，歐美庫存去化以至尾聲，主要的歐洲市
場轉為正成長。不過，中國市場在過去提前消費基期墊
高下，去年下滑 50%，今年仍將下滑 20-30%。為擴展
營運動能公司配合中國「新國標」的安全規範，推出符
合的中低階電動自行車拓展市場。預期 2026 年營收
577.18 億元，YoY -4.2%，EPS 3.18 元。 
五、維持中立理由 
(1)中國市場持續調整 
(2)市場需求仍疲弱

## Document 149: Barclays U.S. Software rAImo's AI Newsletter #108 The AI Math F

Source PDF: all report pdf/Barclays U.S. Software rAImo's AI Newsletter #108 The AI Math F.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-05-22
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

U.S. Software
rAImo's AI Newsletter #108: The AI
Math For The Death Of SaaS Has
Issues In Real Life
We get the current negative software sentiment. However,
what is weird is that many end users of SaaS systems,
especially of the larger platform offerings, somehow don't
seem to share this gloomy outlook. Within, we show their
math which should help investors as well to put things into
perspective.
The debate about the death of software is back in full swing after last week's initial Q1 earnings,

namely from ServiceNow. We get it, we are in very uncertain times, and the new AI capabilities
around agentic and vibe coding do sound exciting.
To ground us in our research work, we often have calls with real world practitioners to make
sure we are not living in a bubble. What is interesting is that their view is a lot more nuanced
compared to the "simple" investors view of the times ahead. Below we describe their thinking.
This should help investors to better level-set the AI and SaaS debate.

For a start, we need to remind ourselves that SaaS companies are often paid out of a company's
IT budget. To get to proper numbers, here we looked at the average IT spending as a percent of
revenue for several industries (in case investors want to do their own math) and at IT spending
as a percentage of revenue over time. See the next two graphs.
Equity Research

We can see in these two graphs that IT spending is a small but not meaningless part of a
company's overall cost base. We can also see that some sectors like Financial Services are more

"tech heavy" than others, which makes sense.
FIGURE 2. Average IT Spending as a Percentage of Revenue For All Sectors

With this knowledge, we can go one step further and can see what a typical (average) S&P
company pays for IT . The table below shows the math, and we are using the median as large
revenue players like Apple (covered by Tim Long) would skew the picture. The median annual

revenue of an S&P 500 company is around $15bn. The corresponding OpEx is around $3bn.
Using the IT spending percentages from the graph above gets us to a typical IT budget around
the $0.5bn range. However, we also know that large banks sit easily above the $1bn range.
27 April 2026 2
Barclays | U.S. Software

### Page 3

FIGURE 3. Summary of Average (Median) S&P 500 Revenue and OpEx and Calculated Median IT Spending
Median Revenue for S&P 500 $14.67bn
Median Operating Expenses for S&P 500 $3.17bn
Average IT Spending as a Percentage of Revenue 2.9%
Calculated Median IT Spending $430mn
Source: Avasant Research, Bloomberg, Barclays Research
This is clearly a fair amount of money that is being spent each year for software (usually about
20% of a typical IT budget), but also hardware (20%) and people (50%). The reason why SaaS

end users have less enthusiasm for this whole AI is killing SaaS theme is that the math is
actually working really well for them. For a little more payment to the SaaS players, they are
saving on hardware and people (the biggest budget item) as the SaaS players provide a
"service" , and hence need to handle all the IT themselves.
However, there is another, even stronger argument. We show this on the example of
ServiceNow. The graph below shows ServiceNow's top customer stats. It has over 600

customers who are spending over $5m in ACV with the company, and the average (not median)
spend is around $15m per year. In other words, about 3% ($14.9m NOW spending divided by the
$430m IT budget [we assume the 630 customers are of the average S&P 500 size or bigger]) of a
company's IT budget is being used to run the whole IT function (NOW's predominant role in the
enterprise), and some add on functions in HR and customer support. Compared to the overall

spending of a company (both for IT and overall OpEx), that is actually a tiny number. This is also
where the push back for many of our industry checks starts. The argument goes like this: "Why
would I create 6-12 months of disruption to my organization, potentially risking the company (in
case things go wrong), to only save a single digit million dollar amount (as the new AI solution
will also cost money) - the math just does not make sense ... " .

27 April 2026 3
Barclays | U.S. Software

### Page 4

FIGURE 4. ServiceNow's Growth in $5M+ ACV Customers and there ACV level (in $mn)
Source: ServiceNow, Barclays Research
The same math is true for the other SaaS platforms as well. We suspect customers are likely
paying more for SAP (but much larger disruption potential in case a swap to a new vendor goes
wrong) and Salesforce (risk of change is also high as revenue numbers are being touched).
27 April 2026 4
Barclays | U.S. Software

### Page 5

Other Relevant AI News/Resources
The next phase of the Microsoft-OpenAI partnership (Microsoft, 4/27/26). "Today, we are
announcing an amended agreement to simplify our partnership and the way we work together,
grounded in flexibility, certainty and a focus on delivering the benefits of AI broadly. The
agreement spells out: Microsoft remains OpenAI’s primary cloud partner, and OpenAI products
will ship first on Azure, unless Microsoft cannot and chooses not to support the necessary

capabilities. OpenAI can now serve all its products to customers across any cloud
provider. Microsoft will continue to have a license to OpenAI IP for models and products through
2032. Microsoft’s license will now be non-exclusive. Microsoft will no longer pay a revenue share
to OpenAI. Revenue share payments from OpenAI to Microsoft continue through 2030,
independent of OpenAI’s technology progress, at the same percentage but subject to a total
cap. Microsoft continues to participate directly in OpenAI’s growth as a major shareholder. "

GitHub Copilot is moving to usage-based billing (GitHub, 4/27/26). "Today, we are announcing
that all GitHub Copilot plans will transition to usage-based billing on June 1, 2026. Instead
of counting premium requests, every Copilot plan will include a monthly allotment of GitHub AI
Credits, with the option for paid plans to purchase additional usage. Usage will be calculated
based on token consumption, including input, output, and cached tokens, using the listed API

rates for each model. This change aligns Copilot pricing with actual usage and is an important
step toward a sustainable, reliable Copilot business and experience for all users. "
OpenAI releases GPT-5.5 amid a shift to rapid-fire AI updates (Fortune, 4/23/26). "OpenAI on
Thursday released its newest AI model, GPT-5.5, to its paid subscribers. The release, coming just
six weeks after the company debuted GPT-5.4, is an extremely fast turnaround that underscores

how fiercely frontier AI labs are competing for enterprise customers, and how their models are
increasingly evolving through continuous, incremental updates. The company also said there
are 4 million active Codex users and 9 million paying business users on ChatGPT . ChatGPT also
has more than 900 million weekly active users and over 50 million subscribers. It no doubt
hopes those figures will undercut a narrative that has been building across social media that

OpenAI has lost traction among consumers and has fallen behind its arch-rival Anthropic in the
race for enterprise customers.
Microsoft to integrate Anthropic's Mythos into its security development program (Yahoo Tech,
4/22/26)  "Microsoft said on Wednesday it plans to embed advanced artificial
intelligence models, including Anthropic's Claude Mythos Preview, into its secure coding
framework, as the company steps up its cybersecurity capabilities. Incorporating the models

into Microsoft's Security Development Lifecycle (SDL) will help identify vulnerabilities and
develop fixes faster, early on in the cycle, the Windows maker said in a blog. Mythos, announced
on April 7, has found "thousands" of major vulnerabilities in operating systems, web browsers
and other software. Its capabilities to code at a high level have given it a potentially
unprecedented ability to identify cybersecurity vulnerabilities and devise ways to exploit them,

experts said. "
China's DeepSeek releases preview of long-awaited V4 model as AI race intensifies (CNBC,
4/24/26). "Chinese artificial intelligence startup DeepSeek on Friday released a preview version
of its long-awaited V4 large language model, allowing users to test its new capabilities and
features. The release comes more than a year after the Hangzhou-based company introduced
its R1 reasoning model, which rocked global tech markets due to its surprising performance and

cost efficiency. Similar to DeepSeek's previous model releases, the latest upgrade is open-
source, allowing developers to download the code, run it locally and modify it in most cases.
The model is available in both a "pro" and a "flash" version, depending on size, with DeepSeek

Sustainable Investing Related Research: There is currently no globally accepted framework or definition (legal, regulatory or otherwise) of, nor

market consensus as to what constitutes a ‘sustainable’ , ‘ESG’ , ‘green’ , ‘climate-friendly’ or an equivalent company, investment, strategy or
consideration or what precise attributes are required to be eligible to be categorised by such terms. This means there are different ways to evaluate a
company or an investment and so different values may be placed on certain sustainability credentials as well as adverse ESG-related impacts of
companies and ESG controversies. The evolving nature of sustainable investing considerations, models and methodologies means it can be

challenging to definitively and universally classify a company or investment under a sustainable investing label and there may be areas where such
companies and investments could improve or where adverse ESG-related impacts or ESG controversies exist. The evolving nature of sustainable
finance related regulations and the development of jurisdiction-specific regulatory criteria also means that there is likely to be a degree of divergence
as to the interpretation of such terms in the market. We expect industry guidance, market practice, and regulations in this field to continue to evolve.

Our survey, administered by
Guidepoint
between April 6
-
16, asked 140 participants to answer a range of questions
about the future of private credit. The survey included a diverse group of financial professionals familiar with the
industry, with 36% representing general partners at alternative funds, 36% limited partners and fund investors, and

28% bankers and lenders. Portfolio/investment manager was the most common job title, representing 41% of
respondents, followed by lender, debt capital markets, banker (23%), fund allocator (7%) and limited partner (6%).
The group was balanced across geographies, with 35% based in Europe, 34% in Asia and 31% in North America.
